**Question**: Compliance specialist — What does account deletion do to personal data? FR8.2, NFR13, and US7.4 require deletion or anonymisation while legally retained financial records stay labelled.
**Reply**: Soft-delete only
**Options**: ["Anonymise + shred key","Hard-delete","Soft-delete only","Delete everything"]

---

## Question Replied
**Timestamp**: 2026-11-07T02:51:21Z
**Event**: QUESTION_REPLIED
**Session**: ce9c0149-10e7-4177-ad88-c52490214c9a
**Question**: Security engineer — Who is the second approver for a support-access grant, and what is the default time bound? FR11.2 and US9.3 require a read-only, time-bound, reason-recorded, audited, second-approved session.
**Reply**: Admin, 60 minutes
**Options**: ["Admin, 60 minutes","Support, 60 minutes","Account owner, 24h","Self-approved, 60 min"]

## Question Replied
**Timestamp**: 2026-11-07T02:51:21Z
**Event**: QUESTION_REPLIED
**Session**: ce9c0149-10e7-4177-ad88-c52490214c9a
**Question**: Compliance specialist — Where is the jurisdiction and age gate enforced? FR8.4 and US7.6 require the gate before any personal data is collected.
**Reply**: Before sign-up
**Options**: ["Before sign-up","At first sign-in","Auth pre-signup hook","Informational only"]

## Question Replied
**Timestamp**: 2026-11-07T02:51:21Z
**Event**: QUESTION_REPLIED
**Session**: ce9c0149-10e7-4177-ad88-c52490214c9a
**Question**: Architect — How is consent recorded? FR8.3 requires consent for marketing and non-essential analytics with no pre-ticked boxes, and C3 exposes current consent by purpose.
**Reply**: State + history
**Options**: ["State + history","Current state only","Append-only events","Marketing only"]

**Timestamp**: 2026-11-07T02:51:34Z
**Event**: QUESTION_ANSWERED
**Stage**: functional-design
**Details**: Q5: C - Soft-delete only; Q6: A - Admin, 60 minutes; Q7: A - Before sign-up; Q8: A - State + history

## Question Replied
**Timestamp**: 2026-11-07T03:04:55Z
**Event**: QUESTION_REPLIED
**Session**: ce9c0149-10e7-4177-ad88-c52490214c9a
**Question**: Contradiction: you chose "Soft-delete only" for Q5, but FR8.2 requires that personal data be deleted or anonymised (NFR13, US7.4 / AC7.4.1), and AC7.4.2 requires retained financial records to be kept under their legal basis. Soft-delete leaves personal data in place indefinitely, so that requirement would not be met. How should account deletion work?
**Reply**: Anonymise + shred key (Recommended)
**Options**: ["Anonymise + shred key (Recommended)","Hard-delete personal rows","Soft-delete, then purge","Keep soft-delete + exception"]

---

## Question Answered
**Timestamp**: 2026-11-07T03:05:11Z
**Event**: QUESTION_ANSWERED
**Stage**: functional-design
**Details**: Q5 revised: A - Anonymise personal fields in every service, keep labelled legal-retention records, and destroy the per-subject crypto-shredding key in Vault

**Reply**: Address them now
